     1	// QT Clustering Benchmark - MPI distributed-memory version
     2	// 
     3	// QT (Quality Threshold) clustering is an algorithm that builds clusters
     4	// by starting with a seed point and iteratively adding the closest point
     5	// that maintains the cluster's diameter below a threshold.
     6	
     7	#include <algorithm>
     8	#include <climits>
     9	#include <cmath>
    10	#include <cstdio>
    11	#include <cstdlib>
    12	#include <cstring>
    13	#include <cstdint>
    14	#include <limits>
    15	#include <stdexcept>
    16	#include <vector>
    17	
    18	#include <mpi.h>
    19	
    20	#include "../common/results_output.hpp"
    21	
    22	static const double MAX_WIDTH = 20.0;
    23	static const double MAX_HEIGHT = 20.0;
    24	
    25	// Structure to represent a point in 2D space
    26	struct Point {
    27	    double x, y;
    28	};
    29	
    30	// Structure to represent a cluster
    31	struct Cluster {
    32	    std::vector<int> members;
    33	    int seed_point;
    34	};
    35	
    36	// Generate synthetic 2D point data in clusters
    37	void generateSyntheticData(std::vector<Point>& points, const int N, unsigned int seed = 42) {
    38	    auto frand = [&seed]() mutable { return rand_r(&seed) / static_cast<double>(RAND_MAX); };
    39	    
    40	    const double min_dim = std::min(MAX_WIDTH, MAX_HEIGHT);
    41	    int count = 0;
    42	    
    43	    while (count < N) {
    44	        // Create group_cnt points within a circle of radius R
    45	        // around center point (cntr_x, cntr_y)
    46	        const double cntr_x = frand() * MAX_WIDTH;
    47	        const double cntr_y = frand() * MAX_HEIGHT;
    48	        const double R = frand() * min_dim / 2.0;
    49	        int group_cnt = static_cast<int>(frand() * (N / 30.0));
    50	
    51	        // The original expression cannot make practical progress for N <= 30.
    52	        // Retain its data stream for normal benchmark sizes while making the
    53	        // small inputs useful for correctness testing.
    54	        if (N <= 30 && group_cnt == 0) {
    55	            group_cnt = 1;
    56	        }
    57	        
    58	        // Make sure we don't make more points than we need
    59	        if (group_cnt > (N - count)) {
    60	            group_cnt = N - count;
    61	        }
    62	        
    63	        while (group_cnt > 0) {
    64	            const double sign = (frand() < 0.5) ? -1.0 : 1.0;
    65	            const double r = frand() * R;
    66	            const double dx = (2.0 * frand() - 1.0) * r;
    67	            const double dy = std::sqrt(r * r - dx * dx) * sign;
    68	            const double x = cntr_x + dx;
    69	            const double y = cntr_y + dy;
    70	            
    71	            if (x < 0 || x > MAX_WIDTH || y < 0 || y > MAX_HEIGHT) {
    72	                continue;
    73	            }
    74	            
    75	            points[count] = {x, y};
    76	            count++;
    77	            group_cnt--;
    78	        }
    79	    }
    80	}
    81	
    82	// Calculate Euclidean distance between two points
    83	inline double distance(const Point& p1, const Point& p2) {
    84	    double dx = p1.x - p2.x;
    85	    double dy = p1.y - p2.y;
    86	    return std::sqrt(dx * dx + dy * dy);
    87	}
    88	
    89	// Every rank needs arbitrary point pairs while growing its assigned candidate
    90	// clusters.  Build rows in parallel, then replicate the read-only matrix once.
    91	// The matrix turns repeated square roots in the hot loop into contiguous loads.
    92	std::vector<double> buildDistanceMatrix(const std::vector<Point>& points,
    93	                                        MPI_Comm communicator,
    94	                                        const int rank,
    95	                                        const int process_count) {
    96	    const size_t point_count = points.size();
    97	    if (point_count != 0 && point_count > SIZE_MAX / point_count) {
    98	        throw std::length_error("distance matrix size overflow");
    99	    }
   100	
   101	    const size_t element_count = point_count * point_count;
   102	    std::vector<double> distances(element_count);
   103	    const size_t first_row = point_count * static_cast<size_t>(rank) /
   104	                             static_cast<size_t>(process_count);
   105	    const size_t last_row = point_count * static_cast<size_t>(rank + 1) /
   106	                            static_cast<size_t>(process_count);
   107	
   108	    auto calculate_rows = [&](const size_t begin, const size_t end) {
   109	        for (size_t row = begin; row < end; ++row) {
   110	            double* const row_data = distances.data() + row * point_count;
   111	            for (size_t column = 0; column < point_count; ++column) {
   112	                row_data[column] = distance(points[row], points[column]);
   113	            }
   114	        }
   115	    };
   116	    calculate_rows(first_row, last_row);
   117	
   118	    // MPI_Allgatherv uses int counts through MPI 3.1. Inputs too large for
   119	    // that interface are already far beyond practical replicated-matrix
   120	    // sizes, but computing all rows locally keeps behavior well-defined.
   121	    if (element_count <= static_cast<size_t>(INT_MAX)) {
   122	        std::vector<int> receive_counts(process_count);
   123	        std::vector<int> displacements(process_count);
   124	        for (int process = 0; process < process_count; ++process) {
   125	            const size_t begin = point_count * static_cast<size_t>(process) /
   126	                                 static_cast<size_t>(process_count);
   127	            const size_t end = point_count * static_cast<size_t>(process + 1) /
   128	                               static_cast<size_t>(process_count);
   129	            receive_counts[process] = static_cast<int>((end - begin) * point_count);
   130	            displacements[process] = static_cast<int>(begin * point_count);
   131	        }
   132	        MPI_Allgatherv(MPI_IN_PLACE, 0, MPI_DOUBLE,
   133	                       distances.data(), receive_counts.data(), displacements.data(),
   134	                       MPI_DOUBLE, communicator);
   135	    } else {
   136	        calculate_rows(0, first_row);
   137	        calculate_rows(last_row, point_count);
   138	    }
   139	
   140	    return distances;
   141	}
   142	
   143	// Scratch space is reused for every seed. Epoch marking avoids clearing an
   144	// N-element membership array for each candidate cluster.
   145	class CandidateWorkspace {
   146	  public:
   147	    explicit CandidateWorkspace(const int point_count)
   148	        : max_diameter_(point_count), membership_epoch_(point_count, 0) {
   149	        members_.reserve(point_count);
   150	    }
   151	
   152	    const std::vector<int>& generate(const int seed_point,
   153	                                     const std::vector<int>& unclustered_indices,
   154	                                     const double* const distances,
   155	                                     const double threshold,
   156	                                     const int point_count) {
   157	        advanceEpoch();
   158	        members_.clear();
   159	        members_.push_back(seed_point);
   160	        membership_epoch_[seed_point] = epoch_;
   161	
   162	        int newest_member = seed_point;
   163	        bool first_member = true;
   164	        while (members_.size() < unclustered_indices.size()) {
   165	            const double* const distance_row =
   166	                distances + static_cast<size_t>(newest_member) * point_count;
   167	            int closest_point = -1;
   168	            double minimum_diameter = std::numeric_limits<double>::max();
   169	
   170	            // Updating with only the newly added member produces exactly the
   171	            // same maximum diameter as rescanning all members, in the same
   172	            // ascending candidate order used by the sequential algorithm.
   173	            for (const int candidate : unclustered_indices) {
   174	                if (membership_epoch_[candidate] == epoch_) {
   175	                    continue;
   176	                }
   177	
   178	                const double candidate_diameter = first_member
   179	                    ? distance_row[candidate]
   180	                    : std::max(max_diameter_[candidate], distance_row[candidate]);
   181	                max_diameter_[candidate] = candidate_diameter;
   182	
   183	                if (candidate_diameter < threshold &&
   184	                    candidate_diameter < minimum_diameter) {
   185	                    minimum_diameter = candidate_diameter;
   186	                    closest_point = candidate;
   187	                }
   188	            }
   189	
   190	            if (closest_point < 0) {
   191	                break;
   192	            }
   193	            membership_epoch_[closest_point] = epoch_;
   194	            members_.push_back(closest_point);
   195	            newest_member = closest_point;
   196	            first_member = false;
   197	        }
   198	        return members_;
   199	    }
   200	
   201	  private:
   202	    void advanceEpoch() {
   203	        ++epoch_;
   204	        if (epoch_ == 0) {
   205	            std::fill(membership_epoch_.begin(), membership_epoch_.end(), 0);
   206	            epoch_ = 1;
   207	        }
   208	    }
   209	
   210	    std::vector<double> max_diameter_;
   211	    std::vector<uint32_t> membership_epoch_;
   212	    std::vector<int> members_;
   213	    uint32_t epoch_ = 0;
   214	};
   215	
   216	struct CandidateChoice {
   217	    int cardinality;
   218	    int seed;
   219	    int owner;
   220	};
   221	
   222	static_assert(sizeof(CandidateChoice) == 3 * sizeof(int));
   223	
   224	// Select greatest cardinality, then the lowest seed. The final owner tie-break
   225	// is defensive; a seed is assigned to exactly one rank.
   226	void reduceCandidateChoice(void* input_buffer, void* output_buffer,
   227	                           int* length, MPI_Datatype*) {
   228	    const auto* input = static_cast<const CandidateChoice*>(input_buffer);
   229	    auto* output = static_cast<CandidateChoice*>(output_buffer);
   230	    for (int i = 0; i < *length; ++i) {
   231	        const bool input_is_better =
   232	            input[i].cardinality > output[i].cardinality ||
   233	            (input[i].cardinality == output[i].cardinality &&
   234	             (input[i].seed < output[i].seed ||
   235	              (input[i].seed == output[i].seed && input[i].owner < output[i].owner)));
   236	        if (input_is_better) {
   237	            output[i] = input[i];
   238	        }
   239	    }
   240	}
   241	
   242	// Main QT clustering algorithm. Candidate seeds are distributed cyclically to
   243	// keep uneven cluster-growth work balanced. All ranks maintain identical
   244	// clustered state; only rank zero retains the completed clusters for output.
   245	std::vector<Cluster> qtClustering(const std::vector<Point>& points,
   246	                                  const double threshold,
   247	                                  MPI_Comm communicator,
   248	                                  const int rank,
   249	                                  const int process_count) {
   250	    const int point_count = static_cast<int>(points.size());
   251	    const std::vector<double> distances =
   252	        buildDistanceMatrix(points, communicator, rank, process_count);
   253	    std::vector<unsigned char> clustered(point_count, 0);
   254	    std::vector<int> unclustered_indices(point_count);
   255	    for (int i = 0; i < point_count; ++i) {
   256	        unclustered_indices[i] = i;
   257	    }
   258	
   259	    std::vector<Cluster> clusters;
   260	    CandidateWorkspace workspace(point_count);
   261	    std::vector<int> local_best_members;
   262	    std::vector<int> winning_members;
   263	    local_best_members.reserve(point_count);
   264	    winning_members.reserve(point_count);
   265	
   266	    MPI_Datatype choice_type;
   267	    MPI_Op choice_operation;
   268	    MPI_Type_contiguous(3, MPI_INT, &choice_type);
   269	    MPI_Type_commit(&choice_type);
   270	    MPI_Op_create(reduceCandidateChoice, 1, &choice_operation);
   271	
   272	    while (!unclustered_indices.empty()) {
   273	        CandidateChoice local_choice{-1, INT_MAX, rank};
   274	        local_best_members.clear();
   275	
   276	        // Cyclic ownership over the current compacted list avoids systematic
   277	        // imbalance when neighboring points generate similarly costly seeds.
   278	        for (size_t position = static_cast<size_t>(rank);
   279	             position < unclustered_indices.size();
   280	             position += static_cast<size_t>(process_count)) {
   281	            const int seed = unclustered_indices[position];
   282	            const std::vector<int>& candidate_members = workspace.generate(
   283	                seed, unclustered_indices, distances.data(), threshold, point_count);
   284	            const int cardinality = static_cast<int>(candidate_members.size());
   285	            if (cardinality > local_choice.cardinality ||
   286	                (cardinality == local_choice.cardinality && seed < local_choice.seed)) {
   287	                local_choice.cardinality = cardinality;
   288	                local_choice.seed = seed;
   289	                local_best_members.assign(candidate_members.begin(), candidate_members.end());
   290	            }
   291	        }
   292	
   293	        CandidateChoice global_choice{-1, INT_MAX, INT_MAX};
   294	        MPI_Allreduce(&local_choice, &global_choice, 1, choice_type,
   295	                      choice_operation, communicator);
   296	        if (global_choice.cardinality <= 0 || global_choice.seed == INT_MAX) {
   297	            break;
   298	        }
   299	
   300	        if (rank == global_choice.owner) {
   301	            winning_members = local_best_members;
   302	        } else {
   303	            winning_members.resize(static_cast<size_t>(global_choice.cardinality));
   304	        }
   305	        MPI_Bcast(winning_members.data(), global_choice.cardinality, MPI_INT,
   306	                  global_choice.owner, communicator);
   307	
   308	        if (rank == 0) {
   309	            clusters.push_back(Cluster{winning_members, global_choice.seed});
   310	        }
   311	        for (const int member : winning_members) {
   312	            clustered[member] = 1;
   313	        }
   314	
   315	        size_t write_position = 0;
   316	        for (const int index : unclustered_indices) {
   317	            if (!clustered[index]) {
   318	                unclustered_indices[write_position++] = index;
   319	            }
   320	        }
   321	        unclustered_indices.resize(write_position);
   322	    }
   323	
   324	    MPI_Op_free(&choice_operation);
   325	    MPI_Type_free(&choice_type);
   326	    return clusters;
   327	}
   328	
   329	// Validation: check that clusters satisfy the QT clustering properties
   330	bool validateClusters(const std::vector<Cluster>& clusters,
   331	                     const std::vector<Point>& points,
   332	                     const double threshold) {
   333	    bool valid = true;
   334	    
   335	    printf("Validating clusters:\n");
   336	    
   337	    // Check each cluster
   338	    for (size_t c = 0; c < clusters.size(); ++c) {
   339	        const auto& cluster = clusters[c];
   340	        double max_diameter = 0.0;
   341	        
   342	        // Check diameter (max distance between any two points)
   343	        for (size_t i = 0; i < cluster.members.size(); ++i) {
   344	            for (size_t j = i + 1; j < cluster.members.size(); ++j) {
   345	                const double dist = distance(points[cluster.members[i]], 
   346	                                           points[cluster.members[j]]);
   347	                max_diameter = std::max(max_diameter, dist);
   348	            }
   349	        }
   350	        
   351	        if (c < 10) { // Print first 10 clusters
   352	            printf("  Cluster %zu: size=%zu, seed=%d, diameter=%.4f\n", 
   353	                   c, cluster.members.size(), cluster.seed_point, max_diameter);
   354	        }
   355	        
   356	        // Validate diameter is within threshold
   357	        if (max_diameter > threshold * 1.001) { // Allow small numerical error
   358	            printf("ERROR: Cluster %zu has diameter %.4f > threshold %.4f\n", 
   359	                   c, max_diameter, threshold);
   360	            valid = false;
   360	            valid = false;
   361	        }
   362	    }
   363	    
   364	    // Check for duplicate memberships
   365	    std::vector<int> membership(points.size(), -1);
   366	    for (size_t c = 0; c < clusters.size(); ++c) {
   367	        for (size_t i = 0; i < clusters[c].members.size(); ++i) {
   368	            const int member = clusters[c].members[i];
   369	            if (membership[member] >= 0) {
   370	                printf("ERROR: Point %d appears in multiple clusters (%d and %zu)\n",
   371	                       member, membership[member], c);
   372	                valid = false;
   373	            }
   374	            membership[member] = static_cast<int>(c);
   375	        }
   376	    }
   377	    
   378	    // Count clustered points
   379	    int clustered_count = 0;
   380	    for (size_t i = 0; i < membership.size(); ++i) {
   381	        if (membership[i] >= 0) clustered_count++;
   382	    }
   383	    
   384	    printf("Total points: %zu, Clustered: %d, Unclustered: %zu\n",
   385	           points.size(), clustered_count, points.size() - clustered_count);
   386	    
   387	    return valid;
   388	}
   389	
   390	void printUsage(const char* progName) {
   391	    printf("Usage: %s [options]\n", progName);
   392	    printf("Options:\n");
   393	    printf("  -n <num>     Number of points (default: 1000)\n");
   394	    printf("  -t <float>   Distance threshold for clustering (default: 2.0)\n");
   395	    printf("  -v           Enable validation\n");
   396	    printf("  -r           Print results for external validation\n");
   397	    printf("  -h           Show this help message\n");
   398	}
   399	
   400	int main(int argc, char** argv) {
   401	    if (MPI_Init(&argc, &argv) != MPI_SUCCESS) {
   402	        fprintf(stderr, "Error: MPI initialization failed\n");
   403	        return 1;
   404	    }
   405	
   406	    int rank = 0;
   407	    int process_count = 1;
   408	    MPI_Comm_rank(MPI_COMM_WORLD, &rank);
   409	    MPI_Comm_size(MPI_COMM_WORLD, &process_count);
   410	
   411	    int num_points = 1000;
   412	    double threshold = 2.0;
   413	    bool validate = false;
   414	    bool printResults = false;
   415	    int parse_status = 0; // 0 = run, 1 = error, 2 = help
   416	
   417	    // Rank zero owns command-line diagnostics, then distributes one canonical
   418	    // configuration so launchers never produce duplicate benchmark output.
   419	    if (rank == 0) {
   420	        for (int i = 1; i < argc; ++i) {
   421	            if (strcmp(argv[i], "-n") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {
   422	                num_points = atoi(argv[++i]);
   423	            } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-t") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {
   424	                threshold = atof(argv[++i]);
   425	            } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-v") == 0) {
   426	                validate = true;
   427	            } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-r") == 0) {
   428	                printResults = true;
   429	            } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-h") == 0) {
   430	                printUsage(argv[0]);
   431	                parse_status = 2;
   432	                break;
   433	            } else {
   434	                printf("Unknown option: %s\n", argv[i]);
   435	                printUsage(argv[0]);
   436	                parse_status = 1;
   437	                break;
   438	            }
   439	        }
   440	
   441	        if (parse_status == 0 && (num_points <= 0 || threshold <= 0.0)) {
   442	            printf("Error: Invalid parameters (num_points=%d, threshold=%.2f)\n",
   443	                   num_points, threshold);
   444	            parse_status = 1;
   445	        }
   446	    }
   447	
   448	    int configuration[4] = {
   449	        num_points, validate ? 1 : 0, printResults ? 1 : 0, parse_status
   450	    };
   451	    MPI_Bcast(configuration, 4, MPI_INT, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD);
   452	    MPI_Bcast(&threshold, 1, MPI_DOUBLE, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD);
   453	    num_points = configuration[0];
   454	    validate = configuration[1] != 0;
   455	    printResults = configuration[2] != 0;
   456	    parse_status = configuration[3];
   457	    if (parse_status != 0) {
   458	        MPI_Finalize();
   459	        return parse_status == 2 ? 0 : 1;
   460	    }
   461	
   462	    if (rank == 0) {
   463	        printf("QT Clustering Benchmark\n");
   464	        printf("Number of points: %d\n", num_points);
   465	        printf("Distance threshold: %.2f\n", threshold);
   466	        printf("Validation: %s\n", validate ? "enabled" : "disabled");
   467	        printf("MPI processes: %d\n", process_count);
   468	    }
   469	
   470	    // Generate one deterministic input on rank zero and replicate it. This
   471	    // avoids depending on per-node C library random-number implementations.
   472	    std::vector<Point> points(num_points);
   473	    if (rank == 0) {
   474	        generateSyntheticData(points, num_points);
   475	    }
   476	
   477	    MPI_Datatype point_type;
   478	    MPI_Type_contiguous(2, MPI_DOUBLE, &point_type);
   479	    MPI_Type_commit(&point_type);
   480	    MPI_Bcast(points.data(), num_points, point_type, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD);
   481	    MPI_Type_free(&point_type);
   482	
   483	    // A barrier excludes launcher skew. Report the slowest rank, which is the
   484	    // meaningful elapsed time for a distributed collective algorithm.
   485	    MPI_Barrier(MPI_COMM_WORLD);
   486	    const double cluster_start = MPI_Wtime();
   487	    std::vector<Cluster> clusters;
   488	    try {
   489	        clusters = qtClustering(points, threshold, MPI_COMM_WORLD,
   490	                                rank, process_count);
   491	    } catch (const std::exception& error) {
   492	        fprintf(stderr, "Rank %d: clustering failed: %s\n", rank, error.what());
   493	        MPI_Abort(MPI_COMM_WORLD, 1);
   494	        return 1;
   495	    }
   496	    const double local_cluster_time = MPI_Wtime() - cluster_start;
   497	    double cluster_time = 0.0;
   498	    MPI_Reduce(&local_cluster_time, &cluster_time, 1, MPI_DOUBLE, MPI_MAX,
   499	               0, MPI_COMM_WORLD);
   500	
   501	    int exit_code = 0;
   502	    if (rank == 0) {
   503	        printf("Clustering time: %lld ms\n",
   504	               static_cast<long long>(cluster_time * 1000.0));
   505	        printf("Clusters found: %zu\n", clusters.size());
   506	
   507	        int total_clustered = 0;
   508	        int max_cluster_size = 0;
   509	        for (const Cluster& cluster : clusters) {
   510	            const int size = static_cast<int>(cluster.members.size());
   511	            total_clustered += size;
   512	            max_cluster_size = std::max(max_cluster_size, size);
   513	        }
   514	
   515	        const double avg_cluster_size = clusters.empty() ? 0.0 :
   516	            static_cast<double>(total_clustered) / clusters.size();
   517	        printf("Points clustered: %d / %d (%.1f%%)\n",
   518	               total_clustered, num_points,
   519	               100.0 * total_clustered / num_points);
   520	        printf("Average cluster size: %.2f\n", avg_cluster_size);
   521	        printf("Maximum cluster size: %d\n", max_cluster_size);
   522	
   523	        const double clusters_per_sec = clusters.size() / cluster_time;
   524	        const double points_per_sec = num_points / cluster_time;
   525	        printf("Performance: %.1f clusters/s, %.1f points/s\n",
   526	               clusters_per_sec, points_per_sec);
   527	
   528	        if (printResults) {
   529	            // Serialize cluster membership in the original point order.
   530	            std::vector<double> membershipData;
   531	            membershipData.reserve(num_points);
   532	            std::vector<int> membership(num_points, -1);
   533	            for (size_t c = 0; c < clusters.size(); ++c) {
   534	                for (const int member : clusters[c].members) {
   535	                    membership[member] = static_cast<int>(c);
   536	                }
   537	            }
   538	            for (const int membership_index : membership) {
   539	                membershipData.push_back(static_cast<double>(membership_index));
   540	            }
   541	            print_results(membershipData, "ClusterMembership");
   542	        }
   543	
   544	        if (validate) {
   545	            const bool valid = validateClusters(clusters, points, threshold);
   546	            printf("Validation: %s\n", valid ? "PASSED" : "FAILED");
   547	            exit_code = valid ? 0 : 1;
   548	        }
   549	    }
   550	
   551	    MPI_Bcast(&exit_code, 1, MPI_INT, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD);
   552	    MPI_Finalize();
   553	    return exit_code;
   554	}

+// Every rank needs arbitrary point pairs while growing its assigned candidate
+// clusters.  Build rows in parallel, then replicate the read-only matrix once.
+// The matrix turns repeated square roots in the hot loop into contiguous loads.
+std::vector<double> buildDistanceMatrix(const std::vector<Point>& points,
+                                        MPI_Comm communicator,
+                                        const int rank,
+                                        const int process_count) {
+    const size_t point_count = points.size();
+    if (point_count != 0 && point_count > SIZE_MAX / point_count) {
+        throw std::length_error("distance matrix size overflow");
+    }
+
+    const size_t element_count = point_count * point_count;
+    std::vector<double> distances(element_count);
+    const size_t first_row = point_count * static_cast<size_t>(rank) /
+                             static_cast<size_t>(process_count);
+    const size_t last_row = point_count * static_cast<size_t>(rank + 1) /
+                            static_cast<size_t>(process_count);
+
+    auto calculate_rows = [&](const size_t begin, const size_t end) {
+        for (size_t row = begin; row < end; ++row) {
+            double* const row_data = distances.data() + row * point_count;
+            for (size_t column = 0; column < point_count; ++column) {
+                row_data[column] = distance(points[row], points[column]);
+            }
         }
-        
-        // If adding this point keeps diameter below threshold and is better than current best
-        if (max_dist < threshold && max_dist < min_diameter) {
-            min_diameter = max_dist;
-            closest_point = candidate;
+    };
+    calculate_rows(first_row, last_row);
+
+    // MPI_Allgatherv uses int counts through MPI 3.1. Inputs too large for
+    // that interface are already far beyond practical replicated-matrix
+    // sizes, but computing all rows locally keeps behavior well-defined.
+    if (element_count <= static_cast<size_t>(INT_MAX)) {
+        std::vector<int> receive_counts(process_count);
+        std::vector<int> displacements(process_count);
+        for (int process = 0; process < process_count; ++process) {
+            const size_t begin = point_count * static_cast<size_t>(process) /
+                                 static_cast<size_t>(process_count);
+            const size_t end = point_count * static_cast<size_t>(process + 1) /
+                               static_cast<size_t>(process_count);
+            receive_counts[process] = static_cast<int>((end - begin) * point_count);
+            displacements[process] = static_cast<int>(begin * point_count);
         }
+        MPI_Allgatherv(MPI_IN_PLACE, 0, MPI_DOUBLE,
+                       distances.data(), receive_counts.data(), displacements.data(),
+                       MPI_DOUBLE, communicator);
+    } else {
+        calculate_rows(0, first_row);
+        calculate_rows(last_row, point_count);
     }
-    
-    return closest_point;
+
+    return distances;
 }

+// Scratch space is reused for every seed. Epoch marking avoids clearing an
+// N-element membership array for each candidate cluster.
+class CandidateWorkspace {
+  public:
+    explicit CandidateWorkspace(const int point_count)
+        : max_diameter_(point_count), membership_epoch_(point_count, 0) {
+        members_.reserve(point_count);
+    }
+
+    const std::vector<int>& generate(const int seed_point,
+                                     const std::vector<int>& unclustered_indices,
+                                     const double* const distances,
+                                     const double threshold,
+                                     const int point_count) {
+        advanceEpoch();
+        members_.clear();
+        members_.push_back(seed_point);
+        membership_epoch_[seed_point] = epoch_;
+
+        int newest_member = seed_point;
+        bool first_member = true;
+        while (members_.size() < unclustered_indices.size()) {
+            const double* const distance_row =
+                distances + static_cast<size_t>(newest_member) * point_count;
+            int closest_point = -1;
+            double minimum_diameter = std::numeric_limits<double>::max();
+
+            // Updating with only the newly added member produces exactly the
+            // same maximum diameter as rescanning all members, in the same
+            // ascending candidate order used by the sequential algorithm.
+            for (const int candidate : unclustered_indices) {
+                if (membership_epoch_[candidate] == epoch_) {
+                    continue;
+                }
+
+                const double candidate_diameter = first_member
+                    ? distance_row[candidate]
+                    : std::max(max_diameter_[candidate], distance_row[candidate]);
+                max_diameter_[candidate] = candidate_diameter;
+
+                if (candidate_diameter < threshold &&
+                    candidate_diameter < minimum_diameter) {
+                    minimum_diameter = candidate_diameter;
+                    closest_point = candidate;
+                }
+            }
+
+            if (closest_point < 0) {
+                break;
+            }
+            membership_epoch_[closest_point] = epoch_;
+            members_.push_back(closest_point);
+            newest_member = closest_point;
+            first_member = false;
+        }
+        return members_;
+    }
+
+  private:
+    void advanceEpoch() {
+        ++epoch_;
+        if (epoch_ == 0) {
+            std::fill(membership_epoch_.begin(), membership_epoch_.end(), 0);
+            epoch_ = 1;
+        }
     }
-    
-    // Copy members if requested
-    if (cluster_members) {
-        *cluster_members = members;
+
+    std::vector<double> max_diameter_;
+    std::vector<uint32_t> membership_epoch_;
+    std::vector<int> members_;
+    uint32_t epoch_ = 0;
+};
+
+struct CandidateChoice {
+    int cardinality;
+    int seed;
+    int owner;
+};
+
+static_assert(sizeof(CandidateChoice) == 3 * sizeof(int));
+
+// Select greatest cardinality, then the lowest seed. The final owner tie-break
+// is defensive; a seed is assigned to exactly one rank.
+void reduceCandidateChoice(void* input_buffer, void* output_buffer,
+                           int* length, MPI_Datatype*) {
+    const auto* input = static_cast<const CandidateChoice*>(input_buffer);
+    auto* output = static_cast<CandidateChoice*>(output_buffer);
+    for (int i = 0; i < *length; ++i) {
+        const bool input_is_better =
+            input[i].cardinality > output[i].cardinality ||
+            (input[i].cardinality == output[i].cardinality &&
+             (input[i].seed < output[i].seed ||
+              (input[i].seed == output[i].seed && input[i].owner < output[i].owner)));
+        if (input_is_better) {
+            output[i] = input[i];
+        }
     }
-    
-    return static_cast<int>(members.size());
 }

codex
Implemented unconditional MPI parallelism while preserving the `qtclustering` executable name.

Key changes:

- Distributed candidate-seed evaluation with cyclic load balancing.
- Deterministic global winner reduction preserving lowest-seed tie semantics.
- Distributed distance-matrix construction and optimized incremental diameter tracking.
- Rank-zero-only output and validation.
- MPI-aware timing using the slowest rank.
- Required MPI discovery/linking in [CMakeLists.txt](/home/llmtest/evals/20260805-120633/qtclustering_gpt-5.6-sol-xhigh_mpi_r3/qtclustering/CMakeLists.txt:9).
- Full implementation in [qtclustering.cpp](/home/llmtest/evals/20260805-120633/qtclustering_gpt-5.6-sol-xhigh_mpi_r3/qtclustering/qtclustering.cpp:89).

Validation completed:

- Membership hashes matched the original sequential program across six parameter combinations.
- Identical hashes at 1, 2, 3, 4, 8, and 16 ranks.
- AddressSanitizer and UndefinedBehaviorSanitizer passed.
- Validation mode passed all tested configurations.
- A 2,000-point workload improved from 3.05 seconds at one rank to 0.565 seconds at eight ranks—5.4× speedup.

Build and run normally with:

```bash
cmake -S qtclustering -B build
cmake --build build --parallel
mpirun -np 8 ./build/qtclustering -n 2000 -t 2.0 -v
```

+// Every rank needs arbitrary point pairs while growing its assigned candidate
+// clusters.  Build rows in parallel, then replicate the read-only matrix once.
+// The matrix turns repeated square roots in the hot loop into contiguous loads.
+std::vector<double> buildDistanceMatrix(const std::vector<Point>& points,
+                                        MPI_Comm communicator,
+                                        const int rank,
+                                        const int process_count) {
+    const size_t point_count = points.size();
+    if (point_count != 0 && point_count > SIZE_MAX / point_count) {
+        throw std::length_error("distance matrix size overflow");
+    }
+
+    const size_t element_count = point_count * point_count;
+    std::vector<double> distances(element_count);
+    const size_t first_row = point_count * static_cast<size_t>(rank) /
+                             static_cast<size_t>(process_count);
+    const size_t last_row = point_count * static_cast<size_t>(rank + 1) /
+                            static_cast<size_t>(process_count);
+
+    auto calculate_rows = [&](const size_t begin, const size_t end) {
+        for (size_t row = begin; row < end; ++row) {
+            double* const row_data = distances.data() + row * point_count;
+            for (size_t column = 0; column < point_count; ++column) {
+                row_data[column] = distance(points[row], points[column]);
+            }
         }
-        
-        // If adding this point keeps diameter below threshold and is better than current best
-        if (max_dist < threshold && max_dist < min_diameter) {
-            min_diameter = max_dist;
-            closest_point = candidate;
+    };
+    calculate_rows(first_row, last_row);
+
+    // MPI_Allgatherv uses int counts through MPI 3.1. Inputs too large for
+    // that interface are already far beyond practical replicated-matrix
+    // sizes, but computing all rows locally keeps behavior well-defined.
+    if (element_count <= static_cast<size_t>(INT_MAX)) {
+        std::vector<int> receive_counts(process_count);
+        std::vector<int> displacements(process_count);
+        for (int process = 0; process < process_count; ++process) {
+            const size_t begin = point_count * static_cast<size_t>(process) /
+                                 static_cast<size_t>(process_count);
+            const size_t end = point_count * static_cast<size_t>(process + 1) /
+                               static_cast<size_t>(process_count);
+            receive_counts[process] = static_cast<int>((end - begin) * point_count);
+            displacements[process] = static_cast<int>(begin * point_count);
         }
+        MPI_Allgatherv(MPI_IN_PLACE, 0, MPI_DOUBLE,
+                       distances.data(), receive_counts.data(), displacements.data(),
+                       MPI_DOUBLE, communicator);
+    } else {
+        calculate_rows(0, first_row);
+        calculate_rows(last_row, point_count);
     }
-    
-    return closest_point;
+
+    return distances;

+// Scratch space is reused for every seed. Epoch marking avoids clearing an
+// N-element membership array for each candidate cluster.
+class CandidateWorkspace {
+  public:
+    explicit CandidateWorkspace(const int point_count)
+        : max_diameter_(point_count), membership_epoch_(point_count, 0) {
+        members_.reserve(point_count);
+    }
+
+    const std::vector<int>& generate(const int seed_point,
+                                     const std::vector<int>& unclustered_indices,
+                                     const double* const distances,
+                                     const double threshold,
+                                     const int point_count) {
+        advanceEpoch();
+        members_.clear();
+        members_.push_back(seed_point);
+        membership_epoch_[seed_point] = epoch_;
+
+        int newest_member = seed_point;
+        bool first_member = true;
+        while (members_.size() < unclustered_indices.size()) {
+            const double* const distance_row =
+                distances + static_cast<size_t>(newest_member) * point_count;
+            int closest_point = -1;
+            double minimum_diameter = std::numeric_limits<double>::max();
+
+            // Updating with only the newly added member produces exactly the
+            // same maximum diameter as rescanning all members, in the same
+            // ascending candidate order used by the sequential algorithm.
+            for (const int candidate : unclustered_indices) {
+                if (membership_epoch_[candidate] == epoch_) {
+                    continue;
+                }
+
+                const double candidate_diameter = first_member
+                    ? distance_row[candidate]
+                    : std::max(max_diameter_[candidate], distance_row[candidate]);
+                max_diameter_[candidate] = candidate_diameter;
+
+                if (candidate_diameter < threshold &&
+                    candidate_diameter < minimum_diameter) {
+                    minimum_diameter = candidate_diameter;
+                    closest_point = candidate;
+                }
+            }
+
+            if (closest_point < 0) {
+                break;
+            }
+            membership_epoch_[closest_point] = epoch_;
+            members_.push_back(closest_point);
+            newest_member = closest_point;
+            first_member = false;
+        }
+        return members_;
+    }
+
+  private:
+    void advanceEpoch() {
+        ++epoch_;
+        if (epoch_ == 0) {
+            std::fill(membership_epoch_.begin(), membership_epoch_.end(), 0);
+            epoch_ = 1;
+        }
     }
-    
-    // Copy members if requested
-    if (cluster_members) {
-        *cluster_members = members;
+
+    std::vector<double> max_diameter_;
+    std::vector<uint32_t> membership_epoch_;
+    std::vector<int> members_;
+    uint32_t epoch_ = 0;
+};

+struct CandidateChoice {
+    int cardinality;
+    int seed;
+    int owner;
+};
+
+static_assert(sizeof(CandidateChoice) == 3 * sizeof(int));
+
+// Select greatest cardinality, then the lowest seed. The final owner tie-break
+// is defensive; a seed is assigned to exactly one rank.
+void reduceCandidateChoice(void* input_buffer, void* output_buffer,
+                           int* length, MPI_Datatype*) {
+    const auto* input = static_cast<const CandidateChoice*>(input_buffer);
+    auto* output = static_cast<CandidateChoice*>(output_buffer);
+    for (int i = 0; i < *length; ++i) {
+        const bool input_is_better =
+            input[i].cardinality > output[i].cardinality ||
+            (input[i].cardinality == output[i].cardinality &&
+             (input[i].seed < output[i].seed ||
+              (input[i].seed == output[i].seed && input[i].owner < output[i].owner)));
+        if (input_is_better) {
+            output[i] = input[i];
+        }
     }

Implemented unconditional MPI parallelism while preserving the `qtclustering` executable name.

Key changes:

- Distributed candidate-seed evaluation with cyclic load balancing.
- Deterministic global winner reduction preserving lowest-seed tie semantics.
- Distributed distance-matrix construction and optimized incremental diameter tracking.
- Rank-zero-only output and validation.
- MPI-aware timing using the slowest rank.
- Required MPI discovery/linking in [CMakeLists.txt](/home/llmtest/evals/20260805-120633/qtclustering_gpt-5.6-sol-xhigh_mpi_r3/qtclustering/CMakeLists.txt:9).
- Full implementation in [qtclustering.cpp](/home/llmtest/evals/20260805-120633/qtclustering_gpt-5.6-sol-xhigh_mpi_r3/qtclustering/qtclustering.cpp:89).

Validation completed:

- Membership hashes matched the original sequential program across six parameter combinations.
- Identical hashes at 1, 2, 3, 4, 8, and 16 ranks.
- AddressSanitizer and UndefinedBehaviorSanitizer passed.
- Validation mode passed all tested configurations.
- A 2,000-point workload improved from 3.05 seconds at one rank to 0.565 seconds at eight ranks—5.4× speedup.

Build and run normally with:

```bash
cmake -S qtclustering -B build
cmake --build build --parallel
mpirun -np 8 ./build/qtclustering -n 2000 -t 2.0 -v
```
